OLED
space atas = 4 mm
L = 23 mm
P = 35 mm

PIR
P = 22,5 mm
L = 22,5 mm
T = 10 mm

PIR kecil
r = 5 mm

SPEAKER
D = 36 mm

PORT TYPE C
L = 3 mm
P = 8,5 mm

SD CARD
P = 15 mm
L = 2 mm

CASE
P = 45 mm
L = 40 mm
T = 50 mm

SHT30D
P = 20 mm 
L = 15 mm

I2C: SDA (6) SCL (7)
DFPlayer Mini TX (2) RX (3)
PIR: 4
TTP Rub: 5 and 10
TTP Mode: 3


ESP32 C3 Mini
MPU6050
OLED 1.3 128x64 SH1106
SHT30D
TTP223 3x
PIR Sensor
DFPlayer Mini

FRAME
1. sleep_01 = 0.5 s
2. sleep_02 = 0.5 s
3. plain_01 = 1 s
4. plain_02 = 0.5 s
5. angry_01 = 1 s
6. angry_02 = 0.5 s
7. sad_01 = 1 s
8. sad_02 = 0.5 s
9. happy_00 = 0.5 s
10. happy_01 = 0.5 s
11. happy_02 = 0.5 s
12. dizzy_01 = 0.5 s
13. dizzy_02 = 0.5 s
14. dizzy_03 = 0.5 s
15. dizzy_04 = 0.5 s
16. hot_01 = 0.5 s
17. hot_02 = 0.5 s
18. freeze_01 = 0.5 s
19. freeze_02 = 0.5 s

STATE
sleep  : loop sleep_01 02 sequential with each frame duration 0.5s while playing sleep.mp3
plain   : loop plain_01 02 sequential with 1s duration for frame 01 and 0.5s for frame 02 without playing any sound
angry   : loop angry_01 02 sequential with 1s duration for frame 01 and 0.5s for frame 02 while playing angry.mp3
sad     : loop sad_01 02 sequential with 1s duration for frame 01 and 0.5s for frame 02 while playing sad.mp3
happy   : play happy_00 for 1s and then loop happy_01 02 sequential with each frame duration 0.5s while playing happy.mp3
dizzy   : loop dizzy_01 02 03 04 sequential with each frame duration 0.5s while playing dizzy.mp3
hot     : looping, play plain_01 for 1s, then hot_01 for 0.5s, then plain_01 for 1s, then hot_02 for 0.5s. while playing hot.mp3
freeze  : looping, play plain_01 for 1s, then loop freeze_01 for 0.2s and freeze_02 for 0.2s for 3 seconds while playing freeze.mp3

EXPLANATION
So the oled will enter sleep if the pir not detect any human or there are no interaction for 1 minutes at the robot Mode
if it detect human then it will enter plain and play greet.mp3, if it being rubbed using TTP223 1 and 2 for 6 seconds from sleep, it play plain_01 for 1s and enter plain for 3 seconds without playing greet.mp3 after that enter happy for 30 seconds and play happy_sleep.mp3
from the plain, if it not being rubbed for 1 minutes, it enter angry for 1 minutes and playing angry.mp3, if while angry being rubbed, enter happy while play angry_happy.mp3
if still not being rubbed after angry 1 minutes, it enter sad for 30 seconds and playing sad.mp3, after 30s sad, it enter plain again, if under 30s while sad being rubbed, enter happy while play sad_happy.mp3
from plain, under 1 minute and being rubbed for 3 seconds, it enter happy for 30s and playing happy.mp3. after happy state, prevent angry or sad for being trigger
for hot and freeze, it can enter from sleep or plain if the SHT30D read the temperature exceed the threshold, loop for 60s and then enter plain for 10s for the sensor to read the average temperature, if it exceed again and it can re enter the hot or freeze
for dizzy, it can enter from any state, so it override all the state so it can directly enter dizzy if MPU detect the tile angle. duration for dizzy loop 30s while playing dizzy.mp3
from sleep untill it sleep again, only allow once angry and sad, so after it sleep it will reset all the state condition to allow to rerun again after it awake. except happy, dizzy, hot and freeze it always allow to rerun

for mp3 mode, it enter using the TTP Mode and be pressed for 5 seconds, after that the ttp(5) became the volume up and for playing next, the ttp(10) became the volume down and for playing previous.
The ttp(5) volume up must press more than 3 seconds to volume up, if under 3 seconds, it play the next sound. That also apply for the volume down logic.
The dfplayer mini for the mp3 mode at one folder called sounds. To enter the robot mode, re press the ttp mode(10) for 5 seconds. The sound for the robots are at folder named robots.
